# Test Traceability: Free Runner and Pro Broadcasting

**Feature**: [spec.md](spec.md)  
**Plan**: [plan.md](plan.md)  
**Defect Log**: [defect-log.md](defect-log.md)  
**Test Summary**: [test-summary.md](test-summary.md)  
**Created**: 2026-10-14  
**Last Updated**: 2026-10-18

All376 primary artifacts are implemented, with their intended Red/Green history retained below. The registry preserves stable artifact IDs, execution owners and historical evidence paths. Current review refactors are behavior-preserving; historical Green entries are not current-source merge qualification. The Quality Gate Results table records that separate state, and every required gate must pass before the five remaining story/full-validation tasks close. Shared BDD/ATDD labels retain one physical execution owner.

## Scenario Coverage Matrix

No sampling; each stable specification scenario executes once in its owner. Each row has one primary source. Supporting source relationships are in Source Coverage Map. Native MCP scenarios are excluded from browser generation by existing inventory routing, rather than duplicated for coverage. Runtime uses a planned feature-to-Jest binding table and scenario-ID completeness assertion; browser has real playwright-bdd step bindings.

| Scenario ID    | Owning Suite | Evidence Roles | Primary Source                                                                                   | Inputs / Classes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Polarity          | Interface / Binding                                                                                                                               | Rationale                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| -------------- | ------------ | -------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| BDD-US1-FR001  | BDD          | BDD, ATDD      | FR-001                                                                                           | an authenticated Free creator; they register a second device                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Positive          | workflow; test/bdd/steps/runner-free-pro-workflows.steps.ts + test/support/mcp/runner-free-pro-probe.ts                                           | The stated observable result is both this behavior and the stakeholder acceptance boundary; both devices remain registered without a purchase.                                                                                                                                              |
| BDD-US1-FR002  | BDD          | BDD, ATDD      | FR-002; test/bdd/features/runner-free-pro.feature; test/bdd/steps/runner-free-pro.steps.ts       | a Free owner with one active clip broadcast; they start live fallback on another device; root bunx playwright test --config playwright.config.ts --project=bdd-chromium /bdd/runner-free-pro.feature.spec.js$ --workers=1 (distinct normal-denial UI feedback owner) | Negative/boundary | workflow; test/bdd/steps/runner-free-pro-workflows.steps.ts + test/support/mcp/runner-free-pro-probe.ts                                           | The stated observable result is both this behavior and the stakeholder acceptance boundary; the second start is rejected with an explanation and the first broadcast continues.                                                                                                             |

| BDD-US1-DEC001 | BDD | BDD, ATDD | FR-002, EC-002 | a Free leased broadcast loses heartbeat while RTMP continues; another device requests start before the final lease deadline and drain margin | Boundary/state | Native workflow | Server dispatch/renewal/recovery/ACK examples use the native workflow owner; actual local expiry and encoded final-frame/physical closure use the native media owner. These exercise distinct server and local-runtime boundaries, each executed once per qualification. |
| BDD-US1-DEC002 | BDD | BDD, ATDD | FR-002, EC-002 | an offline active device is unlinked or deleted; its old token attempts heartbeat then submits its matching closed-output terminal ACK | Boundary/state | Native workflow | Exact example; one owning execution satisfies equivalent behavior and acceptance roles. |

One runtime owner satisfies equivalent behavior and acceptance roles. |

## Status Vocabulary

- Planned: concrete evidence path/binding/command specified; unexecuted.
- Red: failing intended missing behavior observed and retained before code.
- Green: current source evidence passes.
- Blocked: recorded blocker, no inferred success.
- N/A: only with approved applicability rationale; none in this feature.

## Required Checks

- [x] Every US/FR/SC/EC source has mapped evidence and every stable scenario has one owner.
- [x] Scenario IDs and TDD inventory IDs preserved; declared option classes enumerated with no sampling.
- [x] Registry contains commands/state/evidence once; source maps contain relationships only.
- [x] Shared BDD/ATDD intent equivalence is explicit; no duplicate native/browser execution ownership.
- [x] Required quality gates have thresholds/commands/retention; no unapproved exception.
- [ ] Materialize and execute planned feature files, native adapters and browser bindings before corresponding production behavior.
- [ ] Record intended Red/Green/refactor, complete current-source gates and resulting execution totals.
- [ ] Update defect/summary reports with actual evidence; planning cannot satisfy release Go.
